Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any cosmic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 658. The projective resolution has length at most 449 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to cosmic structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 658/449 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on cosmic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 865. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 560 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 865 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cosmic structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 560. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 218.4 (Invariance Principle)
